      <p class="eyebrow">About Clarity Tax Relief</p>
      <h1>Honest IRS Tax Relief, Built Around You, Since 2018</h1>
      <p class="page-updated" style="font-size:.85rem;opacity:.75;margin:.35rem 0 0">Updated <time datetime="2026-08-14">August 14, 2026</time></p>
      <p class="service-lede">Clarity Tax Relief is a tax resolution firm founded to do this work the way it should be done: tell people the truth about their options, charge a flat fee in writing, and put an experienced professional between them and the IRS.</p>

      <div class="answer-box">
        <p><strong>The short version:</strong> Clarity Tax Relief was founded in 2018 by Melissa Ly to help taxpayers resolve IRS and state tax problems without the hype. We represent individuals in all 50 states, quote flat fees up front, and handle the IRS for you with experienced tax professionals.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="post-cta">
        <h2 id="talk-to-a-real-tax-professional-free">Talk to a Real Tax Professional: Free</h2>
        <p>Tell us what the IRS sent you. We'll review your situation, explain your real options in plain English, and quote a flat fee if you decide to move forward — no pressure, no obligation.</p>
        <p class="post-cta-buttons">
          <a href="/#consult" class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">Get My Free Case Review</a>
          <a href="tel:+18888257779" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">Call (888) 825-7779</a>
        </p>
      </div>

      <aside class="key-takeaways">
        <h2 id="key-takeaways">Key takeaways</h2>
        <ul>
          <li>Clarity Tax Relief was founded in 2018 by Melissa Ly, operates from 1551 N Tustin Ave, Santa Ana, CA 92705, and represents individual taxpayers in all 50 states.</li>
          <li>Fees are quoted as flat amounts in writing before any work starts, and the first consultation is free with no obligation to hire the firm.</li>
          <li>Once you sign a power of attorney, IRS calls and letters are routed to the firm instead of to you, so the correspondence stops arriving at your door.</li>
          <li>Every case follows the same three-step path: free consultation, investigation of your IRS account details, then resolution work on the option your facts support.</li>
          <li>The firm is independent and not affiliated with the IRS; its representatives hold the Enrolled Agent credential, the only federally licensed designation with unlimited IRS representation rights.</li>
        </ul>
      </aside>

      <h2 id="why-we-started-clarity-tax-relief">Why We Started Clarity Tax Relief</h2>
      <figure class="founder-video">
        <video class="founder-video-el" controls preload="none" playsinline width="1920" height="1080"
               data-poster="/assets/video/melissa-founder-intro-poster.webp?v=2"
               aria-label="Melissa Ly, Founder &amp; Chief Tax Officer of Clarity Tax Relief, on why the firm never promises a result before reviewing your documents">
          <source src="/assets/video/melissa-founder-intro.mp4" type="video/mp4">
        </video>
        <figcaption><strong>Melissa Ly</strong> · Founder &amp; Chief Tax Officer, Clarity Tax Relief — in her own words (0:30)</figcaption>
        <details class="founder-video-transcript">
          <summary>Read the transcript</summary>
          <p>I'm Melissa. I founded Clarity Tax Relief in 2018. If you're on this page, someone has probably already promised you a specific result before they'd seen a single one of your documents. I want to explain why we won't do that. Nobody can tell you what the IRS will accept before they've looked at your income, your expenses, and what you own. Anyone who quotes you an outcome on a first phone call is guessing.</p>
        </details>
      </figure>
      <div class="about-founder">
        <img src="/assets/melissa-ly-portrait.webp" alt="Melissa Ly, Founder &amp; Chief Tax Officer of Clarity Tax Relief" width="520" height="650" loading="lazy">
        <div class="about-founder-body">
          <p>The tax-relief industry has earned a bad reputation, and it earned it honestly. Too many firms promise "pennies on the dollar," collect a big up-front fee, and disappear. Founder <strong>Melissa Ly</strong> started Clarity Tax Relief in 2018 because taxpayers in a frightening situation deserve the opposite of that — a straight answer and a real plan.</p>
          <blockquote>
            <p>"At Clarity Tax Relief, our mission is simple: provide honest guidance, exceptional service, and effective solutions that help taxpayers regain control of their financial future."</p>
            <cite>Melissa Ly, Founder &amp; Chief Tax Officer</cite>
          </blockquote>
          <p>That mission shows up in small, concrete ways — we'll tell you when you <em>don't</em> need to hire us, we put every fee in writing before you commit, and the person who reviews your case is an experienced tax professional, not a closer working a script.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h2 id="what-makes-clarity-tax-relief-different">What Makes Clarity Tax Relief Different</h2>
      <ul>
        <li><strong>We don't promise outcomes we can't control.</strong> No "guaranteed settlement," no "pennies on the dollar." We tell you what the IRS's own rules make possible for your specific facts, and what they don't.</li>
        <li><strong>Flat fees, in writing, before any work starts.</strong> You'll know the full cost up front. No hourly meter, no surprise invoices.</li>
        <li><strong>You work with experienced tax professionals.</strong> People who deal with the IRS every day handle your case, not a salesperson who hands you off the moment you sign.</li>
        <li><strong>We get between you and the IRS.</strong> With your power of attorney on file, IRS calls and letters come to us, so you can stop bracing for the next envelope.</li>
        <li><strong>Honest from the first call.</strong> Some callers just need 20 minutes of straight answers. If that's you, we'll say so.</li>
      </ul>
      <p>Doing your due diligence? Good. You should. See <a href="/is-clarity-tax-relief-legit/">how to verify Clarity Tax Relief is legitimate</a> (license lookups, our BBB record, published fees) and <a href="/clarity-tax-relief-complaints/">how we handle complaints</a>, including the outside channels you can escalate to. Journalists and editors writing about the firm can take the fast facts, leadership bios and downloadable logo assets from our <a href="/press/">press &amp; media resources</a> page.</p>

      <h2 id="how-we-work">How We Work</h2>
      <p>Every case follows the same simple path — consultation, investigation, then resolution. We start by understanding your situation for free, retrieve and analyze the details of your IRS account, then negotiate the best outcome your facts support: a settlement, a payment plan, penalty removal, or hardship status. You can see the full breakdown on our <a href="/#how-it-works">how it works</a> page, and the specific programs we handle under <a href="/#services">tax relief services</a>.</p>

      <h2 id="who-we-serve">Who We Serve</h2>
      <p>Clarity Tax Relief is based in Santa Ana, California, and represents individual taxpayers in <strong>all 50 states</strong>. Most of the work happens by phone, email, and secure document upload, so your location is rarely a barrier to getting help. Whether you're holding a CP14 balance-due notice or a final <a href="/services/wage-garnishment-release/">wage garnishment</a> threat, the process starts the same way, with a free, confidential review.</p>

      <h2 id="thinking-of-becoming-an-enrolled-agent">Thinking of Becoming an Enrolled Agent?</h2>
      <p>The people who represent taxpayers here hold the Enrolled Agent credential — the
      only federally licensed tax practitioner designation with unlimited rights to
      represent clients before the IRS. If you are working toward it yourself, we publish
      our full <a href="/ea-exam-prep/">EA exam preparation course</a>: three parts, eighteen
      full-length practice exams, and an explanation for every answer choice. We also hire from it — see <a href="/careers/">careers at Clarity</a>.</p>

<h2 id="about-clarity-tax-relief-answered">About Clarity Tax Relief, Answered</h2>
      <details class="faq-item">
        <summary>Is Clarity Tax Relief affiliated with the IRS?</summary>
        <p>No. Clarity Tax Relief is an independent tax resolution firm. We are not affiliated with the IRS or any government agency. We represent taxpayers, not the government, and deal with the IRS on your behalf under a power of attorney you sign.</p>
      </details>
      <details class="faq-item">
        <summary>Where is Clarity Tax Relief located, and who do you serve?</summary>
        <p>Our office is at 1551 N Tustin Ave, Santa Ana, CA 92705, and we represent taxpayers in all 50 states. Most of our work happens by phone, email, and secure document upload, so where you live is rarely a barrier.</p>
      </details>
      <details class="faq-item">
        <summary>What does Clarity Tax Relief charge?</summary>
        <p>We quote flat fees in writing before any work begins, so you know the full cost up front — no hourly surprises and no pressure. The first conversation is a free, no-obligation consultation.</p>
      </details>
      <details class="faq-item">
        <summary>Who will I actually work with at Clarity?</summary>
        <p>You work directly with experienced tax professionals who deal with the IRS every day, not a commissioned salesperson handing you off after you sign. The person who reviews your case is the kind of person who handles it.</p>
      </details>

      <p>Every engagement carries a 30-day money-back guarantee from the date you sign: cancel within 30 days and you get your money back in full. It is not a promise about the tax outcome. Details are in the <a href="/client-bill-of-rights/#guarantee">Client Bill of Rights</a>.</p>
      <p class="results-disclaimer">Results vary based on individual facts and circumstances. Not all taxpayers qualify for settlement or relief programs, and no specific outcome is guaranteed. This page is general information, not tax or legal advice.</p>

      <p class="eyebrow">Our Promise to You</p>
      <h1>Your Client Bill of Rights</h1>
      <p class="page-updated" style="font-size:.85rem;opacity:.75;margin:.35rem 0 0">Updated <time datetime="2026-08-15">August 15, 2026</time></p>
      <p class="service-lede">When you hire Clarity Tax Relief, you&rsquo;re entitled to a set of protections — in plain English, no fine print. These are the standards we hold ourselves to on every case, and you can expect every one of them from your first call to your last.</p>
      <p class="service-lede">If we ever fall short of them, <a href="/clarity-tax-relief-complaints/">here is exactly how to raise a complaint</a>, with us first, and the outside channels available to you. Still checking us out? See <a href="/is-clarity-tax-relief-legit/">is Clarity Tax Relief legit</a>.</p>

      <div class="answer-box">
        <p>Every Clarity Tax Relief client gets an <strong>honest assessment, a flat fee quoted in writing, an experienced tax professional on their case, clear communication, full confidentiality, and a 30-day money-back guarantee</strong>, with no high-pressure tactics and no promise of a specific outcome.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="post-cta">
        <h2 id="ready-to-see-where-you-stand">Ready to See Where You Stand?</h2>
        <p>Start with a free, confidential consultation. We&rsquo;ll walk you through your real options in plain English and tell you honestly whether we can help — no pressure, no obligation.</p>
        <p class="post-cta-buttons">
          <a href="/#consult" class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">Get My Free Consultation</a>
          <a href="tel:+18888257779" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">Call (888) 825-7779</a>
        </p>
      </div>

      <h2 id="1-an-honest-assessment">1. An Honest Assessment</h2>
      <p>We tell you what&rsquo;s realistically possible, not what sells. If we don&rsquo;t think we can help, or if you don&rsquo;t need a firm at all, we&rsquo;ll say so plainly.</p>

      <h2 id="2-flat-fees-in-writing-before-you-pay">2. Flat Fees in Writing, Before You Pay</h2>
      <p>Your fee is quoted in writing before you pay anything, so you decide with the full price in front of you. No hourly meter, no surprise charges halfway through.</p>

      <h2 id="3-an-experienced-tax-professional-on-your-case">3. An Experienced Tax Professional on Your Case</h2>
      <p>An <a href="/our-process/">experienced tax professional</a> handles your case — never a commissioned salesperson chasing a quota. The person reviewing your situation is there to solve your problem.</p>

      <h2 id="4-clear-communication">4. Clear Communication</h2>
      <p>You always know exactly where your case stands. No black box and no chasing us for updates. You get a real point of contact and plain-English answers.</p>

      <h2 id="5-we-deal-with-the-irs-for-you">5. We Deal With the IRS for You</h2>
      <p>Once we&rsquo;re on your case, the IRS calls and letters come to us. You never face the IRS alone, and you can stop dreading the mailbox.</p>

      <h2 id="6-confidentiality">6. Confidentiality</h2>
      <p>Your information is protected and never sold. We treat your financial details with the privacy they deserve, full stop.</p>

      <h2 id="7-no-high-pressure-tactics-no-false-urgency">7. No High-Pressure Tactics, No False Urgency</h2>
      <p>We never use scare tactics, manufactured deadlines, or pushy sales pressure, and we never promise a specific outcome. You get straight information and the time to decide.</p>

      <h2 id="8-the-real-options">8. The Real Options</h2>
      <p>We tell you which IRS programs you actually qualify for, whether that&rsquo;s the <a href="/services/irs-fresh-start-program/">Fresh Start Program</a>, an <a href="/services/offer-in-compromise/">Offer in Compromise</a>, an affordable <a href="/services/irs-payment-plans/">payment plan</a>, <a href="/services/penalty-abatement/">penalty relief</a>, or <a href="/services/currently-not-collectible/">hardship status</a> — based on your facts, not a sales script.</p>

      <h2 id="guarantee">9. A 30-day money-back guarantee</h2>
      <p>If you&rsquo;re not satisfied within your first 30 days, you can cancel for a full refund of what you&rsquo;ve paid. This 30-day window applies to agreements signed on or after October 16, 2026; an agreement signed before then carries the window stated in it. Cancel by completing and returning the Notice of Cancellation form attached to your agreement; any payments you&rsquo;ve made are refunded, and any signed instrument returned, within 10 days of your cancellation notice, and any related security interest is canceled.</p>

      <h2 id="10-your-federal-taxpayer-bill-of-rights">10. Your Federal Taxpayer Bill of Rights</h2>
      <p>On top of our promises, you have ten rights guaranteed by federal law in your dealings with the IRS, and we honor every one of them. Read the official <a href="https://www.irs.gov/taxpayer-bill-of-rights" target="_blank" rel="noopener">IRS Taxpayer Bill of Rights</a> for the full list.</p>

<p class="results-disclaimer">Results vary based on individual facts and circumstances. Not all taxpayers qualify for settlement or relief programs, and no specific outcome is guaranteed. Clarity Tax Relief is not affiliated with the IRS. This page is general information, not tax or legal advice.</p>

        var vid = '';
        try {
          vid = localStorage.getItem('_tu_vid') || '';
          if (!vid) { vid = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 12) + Date.now().toString(36); localStorage.setItem('_tu_vid', vid); }
        } catch (e) { /* storage blocked — send without an id */ }
        function send(event, root, trigger) {
          var body = JSON.stringify({ event: event, tool: root.getAttribute('data-tool'), path: location.pathname, vid: vid, trigger: trigger || '' });
          try {
            if (navigator.sendBeacon && navigator.sendBeacon(API, new Blob([body], { type: 'text/plain' }))) return;

          if (viewed) return;
          var root = document.querySelector('[data-tool]');
          if (root) { viewed = true; send('view', root); }
        }
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', checkView); else checkView();

        var used = false;
        var CONTROLS = 'button,[role="button"],input,select,textarea,label,summary';
        function onEvent(e) {
          if (used) return;
          var el = e.target;
          if (!el || !el.closest) return;
          var root = el.closest('[data-tool]');
          if (!root) return;
          if (e.type === 'click' && !root.hasAttribute('data-tool-any-click') && !el.closest(CONTROLS)) return;
          used = true;
          if (!viewed) { viewed = true; send('view', root); }
          send('use', root, e.type);
        }
        ['click', 'input', 'change', 'submit'].forEach(function (t) {
          document.addEventListener(t, onEvent, true);
        });
      })();

They are usually not worth it for straightforward cases: streamlined online installment agreements (balance ≤ $50,000) cost $22 through the IRS Online Payment Agreement, and First Time Abate can be requested by phone. The FTC warns that most taxpayers don't qualify for the aggressive settlements these companies advertise, so run your specific numbers through our Offer in Compromise calculator before you hire anyone."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Is IRS tax relief legit?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"IRS tax relief itself is real — Offer in Compromise, installment agreements, Currently Not Collectible status, and penalty abatement are all statutory programs the IRS administers under the Internal Revenue Code. The FY2025 IRS Data Book shows 7,199 of 33,591 offers were accepted (21.4%). Tax-relief companies that help clients access these programs are legitimate businesses. What isn't legitimate: firms promising 'pennies on the dollar' before ever seeing your Form 433 financials, or firms that charge upfront fees before doing any work. The FTC's Tax Relief Companies alert covers the specific patterns to avoid."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"How much does a tax relief company cost?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Cost depends on the case type and the firm's pricing model. An Offer in Compromise typically runs $3,000–$7,500 (Enrolled Agents and CPAs at the lower end, tax attorneys at the higher end). A Partial Pay Installment Agreement runs $1,500–$3,500. A First Time Abate request runs $500–$1,500 (though for a First Time Abate, doing it yourself with a phone call to the IRS is usually the right call). Investigation phase fees (used by Community Tax and Optima) start around $295–$549 for individual cases. Flat-fee firms like Precision Tax quote after a free eligibility assessment."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Are any tax relief companies backed by the IRS?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"No. The IRS does not endorse, back, or accredit any private tax-relief company. Any firm that implies IRS endorsement is violating IRS Circular 230 and FTC advertising rules. The IRS's own consumer resources — irs.gov/payments — are free. Some firms use marketing language like 'Fresh Start Program' or 'IRS Approved' that trades on the IRS's actual Fresh Start Initiative (a real 2012 IRS policy change that expanded OIC criteria) but implies more IRS involvement than exists."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Which tax relief company should I use for my case?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Match the firm to your case shape. For balances under $50,000 with straightforward W-2 income, do it yourself through the IRS Online Payment Agreement — no firm needed. For Offer in Compromise cases with W-2 income and modest assets, Precision Tax Relief (flat fee, Enrolled Agents) or Community Tax (lowest investigation fee) are the best value. For business tax problems (payroll, 941, trust fund recovery), Larson Tax Relief is the specialist. For complex cases with criminal exposure, Tax Court potential, or offshore issues, Fortress Tax Relief (attorney-only) is the fit. Our tax attorney vs CPA vs enrolled agent comparison walks through the specific problem shapes each professional handles best."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Can I get my money back if a tax relief company doesn't help me?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"It depends on the firm's guarantee. Anthem Tax Services offers a 100% money-back guarantee on resolution work if they don't reduce your balance or restructure your payment plan (with specific exclusions). Optima Tax Relief has a 15-day money-back guarantee on the investigation phase only. Community Tax refunds the investigation fee if no resolution is possible. Precision Tax Relief has a satisfaction-focused approach but no explicit money-back guarantee posted. Larson and Fortress do not publish money-back guarantees. Read the specific guarantee terms carefully — many exclude bookkeeping, tax prep, and require a minimum deposit."}}]}</script><script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"Person","name":"Jonathan Velez","url":"https://www.themodernwallet.com/about/","jobTitle":"Personal Finance Writer & Editor","description":"Jonathan Velez writes and edits ModernWallet's personal-finance content, focused on loans, interest, and debt payoff. Editor</span> <span class="sep" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-oxbng3yr>·</span> <span class="rev" data-astro-cid-oxbng3yr>Reviewed against primary sources · Updated June 25, 2026</span> </div>   <p class="lede" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>The best tax relief companies have clean BBB records, verifiable staff credentials, transparent pricing, and no recent FTC action. By that standard, there are fewer safe picks in this category than most reviews suggest. We evaluated six national firms based on their years in business, BBB accreditation and complaint volume, staff mix (tax attorneys / CPAs / Enrolled Agents), pricing model, minimum debt threshold, and regulatory history. The FTC's consumer alert on tax-relief companies at consumer.ftc.gov/articles/tax-relief-companies remains live in 2026, and its core guidance is unchanged: don't pay a full fee upfront, most taxpayers don't qualify for the OIC or hardship program advertised, and the recommended first step is a direct payment plan with the IRS.</p><p class="lede" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Before engaging any of the firms below, run your numbers through the <a href="/tax-resolution/">tax resolution calculator</a>, the <a href="/tax-resolution/offer-in-compromise-calculator/">Offer in Compromise calculator</a>, and the <a href="/tax-resolution/irs-payment-plan-calculator/">IRS payment plan calculator</a>. Most cases at $50,000 or less don't need any of these companies at all, and knowing what the IRS's own math shows can keep you from overpaying.</p> </header> <section class="section criteria-section" aria-label="How we ranked" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>How we ranked these tax relief companies</h2> <p>Rankings weighted: years in business (10%), BBB rating and complaint volume (25%), verified staff credentials (20%), pricing transparency (15%), minimum debt threshold and case fit (15%), regulatory history — FTC actions, class actions, state bar complaints (15%). Optima Tax Relief is included despite the 2022 FTC settlement to give readers the full picture; the settlement is disclosed in its listing below.</p> </section> <section class="section option-section" id="precision-tax-relief" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><span class="opt-rank" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>#1</span> Precision Tax Relief</h2> <p class="opt-bestfor" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><strong data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Best for:</strong> Best overall — clean record, longest track, flat-fee pricing</p> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Coeur d'Alene, Idaho, founded 1967 (roughly 59 years in business). A+ BBB rating, accredited since 2011, and only 2 BBB complaints in the last 3 years — the cleanest complaint record among the six national firms reviewed.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Precision Tax staffs Enrolled Agents (federally licensed IRS specialists) rather than attorneys or CPAs — the same unlimited practice rights before the IRS at typically lower cost. Precision has been named 'Best Overall' by Investopedia across multiple years and 'Best Customer Service' by CNBC Select in 2024.</p> <div class="pros-cons" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <div class="pros" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Strengths</h3> <ul data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Cleanest complaint record among national tax-relief firms (2 BBB complaints in 3 years)</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Longest track record in the category — 59 years in business</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Flat-fee pricing with a 'Fair Quote Guarantee' — no phased or hidden fees</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Enrolled Agents on staff (federally licensed IRS specialists)</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Payment plans available for the fee itself</li></ul> </div> <div class="cons" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Limitations</h3> <ul data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>No attorneys on staff — refers out for criminal exposure, Tax Court, or complex Innocent Spouse cases</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Case fees not published on the website — quoted after free eligibility assessment</li></ul> </div> </div> <p class="opt-pricing" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><strong data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Pricing:</strong> Flat fee (custom-quoted per case); payment plans available. Case-fee ranges not published.</p> </section><section class="section option-section" id="community-tax" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><span class="opt-rank" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>#2</span> Community Tax</h2> <p class="opt-bestfor" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><strong data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Best for:</strong> Best for smaller balances — lowest investigation fee</p> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Chicago, Illinois, founded 2010 (approximately 16 years in business). A+ BBB rating, accredited.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Staffs tax attorneys, CPAs, and Enrolled Agents. Community Tax uses a two-phase pricing model that's transparent on the initial investigation fee: roughly $295–$549 for individual cases and $595–$999 for business cases.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>A resolution fee follows the investigation phase, quoted after review. Community Tax refunds the investigation fee if no resolution is possible, which is unusual in the category.</p> <div class="pros-cons" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <div class="pros" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Strengths</h3> <ul data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Lowest published investigation fee in the category ($295–$549 individual)</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Investigation-fee refund if no resolution is possible</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>All three professional credentials on staff</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Minimum debt threshold around $7,000–$10,000 — accepts smaller cases than most competitors</li></ul> </div> <div class="cons" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Limitations</h3> <ul data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Resolution-phase fees are not published — quoted after investigation</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Standard service-quality complaints in BBB history (typical for the category)</li></ul> </div> </div> <p class="opt-pricing" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><strong data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Pricing:</strong> Two-phase: investigation fee $295–$549 individual ($595–$999 business) + resolution fee quoted after review.</p> </section><section class="section option-section" id="larson-tax-relief" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><span class="opt-rank" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>#3</span> Larson Tax Relief</h2> <p class="opt-bestfor" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><strong data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Best for:</strong> Best for business tax problems (payroll, 941, business levies)</p> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Westminster, Colorado, family-owned for over 20 years. A+ BBB rating, accredited.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Larson is unusual in the category for its $25,000 minimum debt threshold — higher than every other national firm — and its business-tax specialty. Roughly 80% of Larson's cases are payroll tax issues (Form 941), IRS levies on operating businesses, and trust fund recovery penalty cases.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Very low complaint volume, no FTC or class-action history.</p> <div class="pros-cons" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <div class="pros" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Strengths</h3> <ul data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Business tax specialist — 80% of cases are payroll/business tax</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Very low BBB complaint volume across 20+ years</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>All three professional credentials on staff</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Family-owned and stable</li></ul> </div> <div class="cons" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Limitations</h3> <ul data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>$25,000 minimum debt threshold — won't take smaller individual cases</li><li data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Fees custom-quoted; higher than category norm given business-case focus</li></ul> </div> </div> <p class="opt-pricing" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><strong data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Pricing:</strong> Flat fee, custom-quoted per case. $25,000 minimum debt.</p> </section><section class="section option-section" id="fortress-tax-relief" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><span class="opt-rank" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>#4</span> Fortress Tax Relief</h2> <p class="opt-bestfor" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><strong data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Best for:</strong> Best for complex or high-dollar attorney-required cases</p> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Bend, Oregon. </p> </section>  <aside class="sponsor-slot" aria-label="Advertise with us" data-astro-cid-fxpisvfz> <span class="sponsor-slot__icon" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-fxpisvfz>📣</span> <div class="sponsor-slot__copy" data-astro-cid-fxpisvfz> <p class="sponsor-slot__title" data-astro-cid-fxpisvfz>Building in finance?</p> <p class="sponsor-slot__text" data-astro-cid-fxpisvfz>Put your product in front of readers comparing options on this page.</p> </div> <a class="sponsor-slot__btn" href="/sponsor?from=%2Froundup%2Fbest-tax-relief-companies%2F" rel="nofollow" data-astro-cid-fxpisvfz>Advertise With Us →</a> </aside>  <section class="section verdict-section" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Our verdict: which should you choose?</h2> <div class="verdict-box" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Precision Tax Relief is the best overall pick for most taxpayers — the cleanest complaint record, the longest track record, flat-fee pricing, and Enrolled Agents at rates below attorney-heavy firms. Community Tax is the right choice when your balance is on the smaller side ($7,000–$20,000) and you want the lowest published investigation fee.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Larson Tax Relief is the pick for business tax problems (payroll, 941, business levies). Fortress Tax Relief is the pick when your case has criminal exposure, Tax Court potential, or complex Innocent Spouse facts that require attorney-client privilege.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Anthem Tax Services is worth considering only if the money-back guarantee is the decisive factor for you. Optima Tax Relief is included for completeness — the 2022 FTC settlement and elevated complaint volume mean the burden of proof is on you to justify choosing them over the alternatives above.</p><p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Before you hire any of these firms, run your specific numbers through our <a href="/tax-resolution/">tax resolution hub</a> — most straightforward cases (streamlined installment agreements, First Time Abate) don't need a paid firm at all.</p> </div> </section> <section class="section" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>How to avoid tax-relief scams (FTC guidance)</h2> <p>The FTC's consumer alert at consumer.ftc.gov/articles/tax-relief-companies has stayed live and unchanged in substance for years. The core warnings: (1) don't pay a full fee upfront; (2) no company can guarantee a specific outcome — Offer in Compromise acceptance rates are around 21% (7,199 of 33,591 in FY2025 per the IRS Data Book), not the 90%+ some ads suggest; (3) monthly 'maintenance fees' often stretch cases out for months or years without meaningful progress; (4) the IRS's own Online Payment Agreement for balances under $50,000 costs $22 to set up and doesn't need a paid firm at all.</p><p>A practical filter: if a company won't tell you their fee before running your Reasonable Collection Potential math, walk away. Our <a href="/tax-resolution/offer-in-compromise-calculator/">Offer in Compromise calculator</a> shows exactly what the IRS's Form 656-B worksheet returns for your specific numbers — a legitimate firm can match or improve on that, and shouldn't want to hide the underlying math.</p> </section><section class="section" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>When you don&#39;t need a tax-relief company</h2> <p>Most straightforward IRS collection cases don't require any of the firms in this roundup. A streamlined online installment agreement for balances of $50,000 or less costs $22 to set up (direct debit) and can be done through the IRS's Online Payment Agreement application in about 15 minutes. First Time Abate is available via a phone call to the toll-free number on your penalty notice if you've been penalty-free for the last 3 years. Currently Not Collectible status uses Form 433-F and is filed directly with the IRS.</p><p>Hiring a professional makes sense when the case has real complexity: an Offer in Compromise (the RCP math is nuanced), a Partial Pay Installment Agreement (Form 433-F financials + 2-year mandatory reviews), a Collection Due Process appeal after Letter 1058, or an Innocent Spouse Relief case. For those, an Enrolled Agent ($150–$300/hour, $3,000–$7,500 for OIC) is usually the right hire — see our <a href="/compare/tax-attorney-vs-cpa-vs-enrolled-agent/">tax attorney vs CPA vs enrolled agent comparison</a> for the specific problem-shape guidance.</p> </section><section class="section" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>How we ranked</h2> <p>Rankings weighted years in business (10%), BBB rating and complaint volume (25%), verified staff credentials (20%), pricing transparency (15%), minimum debt threshold and case fit (15%), and regulatory history including FTC actions, class actions, and state bar complaints (15%). Optima Tax Relief was included despite its 2022 FTC settlement to give readers the full picture; the settlement and elevated complaint volume are disclosed in its listing. Where a firm did not publish specific fees or credential mixes, we noted the gap rather than importing third-party estimates.</p> </section> <section class="section" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Frequently asked questions</h2> <div class="faqs" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <details class="faq" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <summary data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Are tax relief companies worth it?</h3></summary> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Tax relief companies are worth it for genuinely complex cases — Offer in Compromise prep, Partial Pay Installment Agreements, Collection Due Process appeals, and Innocent Spouse Relief — where the numbers and paperwork justify a $3,000–$7,500 professional fee. They are usually not worth it for straightforward cases: streamlined online installment agreements (balance ≤ $50,000) cost $22 through the IRS Online Payment Agreement, and First Time Abate can be requested by phone. The FTC warns that most taxpayers don't qualify for the aggressive settlements these companies advertise, so run your specific numbers through our <a href="/tax-resolution/offer-in-compromise-calculator/">Offer in Compromise calculator</a> before you hire anyone.</p> </details><details class="faq" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <summary data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Is IRS tax relief legit?</h3></summary> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>IRS tax relief itself is real — Offer in Compromise, installment agreements, Currently Not Collectible status, and penalty abatement are all statutory programs the IRS administers under the Internal Revenue Code. The FY2025 IRS Data Book shows 7,199 of 33,591 offers were accepted (21.4%). Tax-relief companies that help clients access these programs are legitimate businesses. What isn't legitimate: firms promising 'pennies on the dollar' before ever seeing your Form 433 financials, or firms that charge upfront fees before doing any work. The FTC's Tax Relief Companies alert covers the specific patterns to avoid.</p> </details><details class="faq" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <summary data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>How much does a tax relief company cost?</h3></summary> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Cost depends on the case type and the firm's pricing model. An Offer in Compromise typically runs $3,000–$7,500 (Enrolled Agents and CPAs at the lower end, tax attorneys at the higher end). A Partial Pay Installment Agreement runs $1,500–$3,500. A First Time Abate request runs $500–$1,500 (though for a First Time Abate, doing it yourself with a phone call to the IRS is usually the right call). Investigation phase fees (used by Community Tax and Optima) start around $295–$549 for individual cases. Flat-fee firms like Precision Tax quote after a free eligibility assessment.</p> </details><details class="faq" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <summary data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Are any tax relief companies backed by the IRS?</h3></summary> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>No. The IRS does not endorse, back, or accredit any private tax-relief company. Any firm that implies IRS endorsement is violating IRS Circular 230 and FTC advertising rules. The IRS's own consumer resources — irs.gov/payments — are free. Some firms use marketing language like 'Fresh Start Program' or 'IRS Approved' that trades on the IRS's actual Fresh Start Initiative (a real 2012 IRS policy change that expanded OIC criteria) but implies more IRS involvement than exists.</p> </details><details class="faq" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <summary data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Which tax relief company should I use for my case?</h3></summary> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Match the firm to your case shape. For balances under $50,000 with straightforward W-2 income, do it yourself through the IRS Online Payment Agreement — no firm needed. For Offer in Compromise cases with W-2 income and modest assets, Precision Tax Relief (flat fee, Enrolled Agents) or Community Tax (lowest investigation fee) are the best value. For business tax problems (payroll, 941, trust fund recovery), Larson Tax Relief is the specialist. For complex cases with criminal exposure, Tax Court potential, or offshore issues, Fortress Tax Relief (attorney-only) is the fit. Our <a href="/compare/tax-attorney-vs-cpa-vs-enrolled-agent/">tax attorney vs CPA vs enrolled agent comparison</a> walks through the specific problem shapes each professional handles best.</p> </details><details class="faq" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <summary data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l><h3 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Can I get my money back if a tax relief company doesn&#39;t help me?</h3></summary> <p data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>It depends on the firm's guarantee. Anthem Tax Services offers a 100% money-back guarantee on resolution work if they don't reduce your balance or restructure your payment plan (with specific exclusions). Optima Tax Relief has a 15-day money-back guarantee on the investigation phase only. Community Tax refunds the investigation fee if no resolution is possible. Precision Tax Relief has a satisfaction-focused approach but no explicit money-back guarantee posted. Larson and Fortress do not publish money-back guarantees. Read the specific guarantee terms carefully — many exclude bookkeeping, tax prep, and require a minimum deposit.</p> </details> </div> </section> <section class="section cta-section" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <h2 data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Free calculators to help you decide</h2> <div class="cta-grid" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <a href="/tax-resolution/" class="cta-card" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <span class="cta-label" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Tax resolution calculator</span> <span class="cta-arrow" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>→</span> </a><a href="/tax-resolution/offer-in-compromise-calculator/" class="cta-card" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <span class="cta-label" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Offer in compromise calculator</span> <span class="cta-arrow" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>→</span> </a><a href="/tax-resolution/irs-payment-plan-calculator/" class="cta-card" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <span class="cta-label" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>IRS payment plan calculator</span> <span class="cta-arrow" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>→</span> </a><a href="/tax-resolution/penalty-abatement-calculator/" class="cta-card" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l> <span class="cta-label" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>Penalty abatement calculator</span> <span class="cta-arrow" data-astro-cid-nx7ly24l>→</span> </a> </div> </section> <section class="section sources" data-astro-cid-3ntd4h4x><h2 data-astro-cid-3ntd4h4x>Sources</h2><p class="source-note" data-astro-cid-3ntd4h4x>

By that standard, there are fewer safe picks in this category than most reviews suggest. We evaluated six national firms based on their years in business, BBB accreditation and complaint volume, staff mix (tax attorneys / CPAs / Enrolled Agents), pricing model, minimum debt threshold, and regulatory history. The FTC's consumer alert on tax-relief companies at consumer.ftc.gov/articles/tax-relief-companies remains live in 2026, and its core guidance is unchanged: don't pay a full fee upfront, most taxpayers don't qualify for the OIC or hardship program advertised, and the recommended first step is a direct payment plan with the IRS. Before engaging any of the firms below, run your numbers through the tax resolution calculator , the Offer in Compromise calculator , and the IRS payment plan calculator . Most cases at $50,000 or less don't need any of these companies at all, and knowing what the IRS's own math shows can keep you from overpaying. How we ranked these tax relief companies Rankings weighted: years in business (10%), BBB rating and complaint volume (25%), verified staff credentials (20%), pricing transparency (15%), minimum debt threshold and case fit (15%), regulatory history — FTC actions, class actions, state bar complaints (15%). Optima Tax Relief is included despite the 2022 FTC settlement to give readers the full picture; the settlement is disclosed in its listing below. #1 Precision Tax Relief Best for: Best overall — clean record, longest track, flat-fee pricing Coeur d'Alene, Idaho, founded 1967 (roughly 59 years in business). A+ BBB rating, accredited since 2011, and only 2 BBB complaints in the last 3 years — the cleanest complaint record among the six national firms reviewed. Precision Tax staffs Enrolled Agents (federally licensed IRS specialists) rather than attorneys or CPAs — the same unlimited practice rights before the IRS at typically lower cost. Precision has been named 'Best Overall' by Investopedia across multiple years and 'Best Customer Service' by CNBC Select in 2024. Strengths Cleanest complaint record among national tax-relief firms (2 BBB complaints in 3 years) Longest track record in the category — 59 years in business Flat-fee pricing with a 'Fair Quote Guarantee' — no phased or hidden fees Enrolled Agents on staff (federally licensed IRS specialists) Payment plans available for the fee itself Limitations No attorneys on staff — refers out for criminal exposure, Tax Court, or complex Innocent Spouse cases Case fees not published on the website — quoted after free eligibility assessment Pricing: Flat fee (custom-quoted per case); payment plans available. Free eligibility assessment. Case-fee ranges not published. #2 Community Tax Best for: Best for smaller balances — lowest investigation fee Chicago, Illinois, founded 2010 (approximately 16 years in business). A+ BBB rating, accredited. Staffs tax attorneys, CPAs, and Enrolled Agents. Community Tax uses a two-phase pricing model that's transparent on the initial investigation fee: roughly $295–$549 for individual cases and $595–$999 for business cases. A resolution fee follows the investigation phase, quoted after review. Community Tax refunds the investigation fee if no resolution is possible, which is unusual in the category. Strengths Lowest published investigation fee in the category ($295–$549 individual) Investigation-fee refund if no resolution is possible All three professional credentials on staff Minimum debt threshold around $7,000–$10,000 — accepts smaller cases than most competitors Limitations Resolution-phase fees are not published — quoted after investigation Standard service-quality complaints in BBB history (typical for the category) Pricing: Two-phase: investigation fee $295–$549 individual ($595–$999 business) + resolution fee quoted after review. #3 Larson Tax Relief Best for: Best for business tax problems (payroll, 941, business levies) Westminster, Colorado, family-owned for over 20 years. A+ BBB rating, accredited. Larson is unusual in the category for its $25,000 minimum debt threshold — higher than every other national firm — and its business-tax specialty. Roughly 80% of Larson's cases are payroll tax issues (Form 941), IRS levies on operating businesses, and trust fund recovery penalty cases. Very low complaint volume, no FTC or class-action history. Strengths Business tax specialist — 80% of cases are payroll/business tax Very low BBB complaint volume across 20+ years All three professional credentials on staff Family-owned and stable Limitations $25,000 minimum debt threshold — won't take smaller individual cases Fees custom-quoted; higher than category norm given business-case focus Pricing: Flat fee, custom-quoted per case. $25,000 minimum debt. #4 Fortress Tax Relief Best for: Best for complex or high-dollar attorney-required cases Bend, Oregon. Roughly 20 years in business. A+ BBB rating. Fortress is the only national firm reviewed where every case is worked by a licensed tax attorney — no sales-rep intake or case-manager handling. That makes it a fit for cases with criminal exposure, Tax Court petitions, complex Innocent Spouse Relief facts, or offshore reporting issues where attorney-client privilege matters. $20,000 minimum debt. Strengths Every case worked by a licensed tax attorney (attorney-client privilege applies) Handles cases up to $20 million Clean regulatory history — no FTC or class-action issues Fit for cases where CPAs/EAs cannot go (criminal, Tax Court, complex Innocent Spouse) Limitations Hourly billing rather than flat fee — higher cost on straightforward cases $20,000 minimum debt threshold Fees not published on the site — attorney rates typically $300–$600+/hour Pricing: Hourly attorney billing; $20,000 minimum debt threshold. Rates not published; attorney category rates apply. #5 Anthem Tax Services Best for: Best money-back guarantee Woodland Hills, California, founded 2010 (roughly 16 years in business). A+ BBB rating, accredited since February 2017. Anthem offers a 100% money-back guarantee on resolution work if the firm doesn't reduce the balance owed or restructure the payment plan (the guarantee excludes bookkeeping, tax prep, and a required minimum deposit). Staff credentials are not itemized specifically on the public site. Strengths 100% money-back guarantee on resolution work A+ BBB accreditation since 2017 16 years in business Limitations Minimum debt threshold not disclosed on the site Staff credential mix not itemized publicly (BBB shows 'tax professionals' without breakdown) Fees not disclosed on the site — quoted after consultation Pricing: Not disclosed on site. Money-back guarantee on resolution work with defined exclusions. #6 Optima Tax Relief Best for: Largest scale — but read the FTC-settlement disclosure Santa Ana, California, founded 2011 (roughly 15 years in business). A+ BBB rating, accredited. Largest firm by resolved-dollars claimed ($3 billion+). REGULATORY DISCLOSURE: Optima settled with the FTC in 2022 for $7 million in consumer redress (part of a broader $12 million package) over allegations of misrepresenting savings and refund practices. Optima has continued operating and rebuilt its process, but BBB complaint volume remains high (770+ in the last 3 years). If you're considering Optima, factor in the FTC history and heightened complaint volume against its scale and brand recognition. Advertise With Us → Our verdict: which should you choose? Precision Tax Relief is the best overall pick for most taxpayers — the cleanest complaint record, the longest track record, flat-fee pricing, and Enrolled Agents at rates below attorney-heavy firms. Community Tax is the right choice when your balance is on the smaller side ($7,000–$20,000) and you want the lowest published investigation fee. Larson Tax Relief is the pick for business tax problems (payroll, 941, business levies). Fortress Tax Relief is the pick when your case has criminal exposure, Tax Court potential, or complex Innocent Spouse facts that require attorney-client privilege. Anthem Tax Services is worth considering only if the money-back guarantee is the decisive factor for you. Optima Tax Relief is included for completeness — the 2022 FTC settlement and elevated complaint volume mean the burden of proof is on you to justify choosing them over the alternatives above. Before you hire any of these firms, run your specific numbers through our tax resolution hub — most straightforward cases (streamlined installment agreements, First Time Abate) don't need a paid firm at all. How to avoid tax-relief scams (FTC guidance) The FTC's consumer alert at consumer.ftc.gov/articles/tax-relief-companies has stayed live and unchanged in substance for years. The core warnings: (1) don't pay a full fee upfront; (2) no company can guarantee a specific outcome — Offer in Compromise acceptance rates are around 21% (7,199 of 33,591 in FY2025 per the IRS Data Book), not the 90%+ some ads suggest; (3) monthly 'maintenance fees' often stretch cases out for months or years without meaningful progress; (4) the IRS's own Online Payment Agreement for balances under $50,000 costs $22 to set up and doesn't need a paid firm at all. A practical filter: if a company won't tell you their fee before running your Reasonable Collection Potential math, walk away. Our Offer in Compromise calculator shows exactly what the IRS's Form 656-B worksheet returns for your specific numbers — a legitimate firm can match or improve on that, and shouldn't want to hide the underlying math. When you don't need a tax-relief company Most straightforward IRS collection cases don't require any of the firms in this roundup. A streamlined online installment agreement for balances of $50,000 or less costs $22 to set up (direct debit) and can be done through the IRS's Online Payment Agreement application in about 15 minutes. First Time Abate is available via a phone call to the toll-free number on your penalty notice if you've been penalty-free for the last 3 years. Currently Not Collectible status uses Form 433-F and is filed directly with the IRS. Hiring a professional makes sense when the case has real complexity: an Offer in Compromise (the RCP math is nuanced), a Partial Pay Installment Agreement (Form 433-F financials + 2-year mandatory reviews), a Collection Due Process appeal after Letter 1058, or an Innocent Spouse Relief case. For those, an Enrolled Agent ($150–$300/hour, $3,000–$7,500 for OIC) is usually the right hire — see our tax attorney vs CPA vs enrolled agent comparison for the specific problem-shape guidance. How we ranked Rankings weighted years in business (10%), BBB rating and complaint volume (25%), verified staff credentials (20%), pricing transparency (15%), minimum debt threshold and case fit (15%), and regulatory history including FTC actions, class actions, and state bar complaints (15%). Optima Tax Relief was included despite its 2022 FTC settlement to give readers the full picture; the settlement and elevated complaint volume are disclosed in its listing. Where a firm did not publish specific fees or credential mixes, we noted the gap rather than importing third-party estimates. Frequently asked questions Are tax relief companies worth it? Tax relief companies are worth it for genuinely complex cases — Offer in Compromise prep, Partial Pay Installment Agreements, Collection Due Process appeals, and Innocent Spouse Relief — where the numbers and paperwork justify a $3,000–$7,500 professional fee. They are usually not worth it for straightforward cases: streamlined online installment agreements (balance ≤ $50,000) cost $22 through the IRS Online Payment Agreement, and First Time Abate can be requested by phone. The FTC warns that most taxpayers don't qualify for the aggressive settlements these companies advertise, so run your specific numbers through our Offer in Compromise calculator before you hire anyone. Is IRS tax relief legit? IRS tax relief itself is real — Offer in Compromise, installment agreements, Currently Not Collectible status, and penalty abatement are all statutory programs the IRS administers under the Internal Revenue Code. The FY2025 IRS Data Book shows 7,199 of 33,591 offers were accepted (21.4%). Tax-relief companies that help clients access these programs are legitimate businesses. What isn't legitimate: firms promising 'pennies on the dollar' before ever seeing your Form 433 financials, or firms that charge upfront fees before doing any work. The FTC's Tax Relief Companies alert covers the specific patterns to avoid. How much does a tax relief company cost? Cost depends on the case type and the firm's pricing model. An Offer in Compromise typically runs $3,000–$7,500 (Enrolled Agents and CPAs at the lower end, tax attorneys at the higher end). A Partial Pay Installment Agreement runs $1,500–$3,500. A First Time Abate request runs $500–$1,500 (though for a First Time Abate, doing it yourself with a phone call to the IRS is usually the right call). Investigation phase fees (used by Community Tax and Optima) start around $295–$549 for individual cases. Flat-fee firms like Precision Tax quote after a free eligibility assessment. Are any tax relief companies backed by the IRS? No. The IRS does not endorse, back, or accredit any private tax-relief company. Any firm that implies IRS endorsement is violating IRS Circular 230 and FTC advertising rules. The IRS's own consumer resources — irs.gov/payments — are free. Some firms use marketing language like 'Fresh Start Program' or 'IRS Approved' that trades on the IRS's actual Fresh Start Initiative (a real 2012 IRS policy change that expanded OIC criteria) but implies more IRS involvement than exists. Which tax relief company should I use for my case? Match the firm to your case shape. For balances under $50,000 with straightforward W-2 income, do it yourself through the IRS Online Payment Agreement — no firm needed. For Offer in Compromise cases with W-2 income and modest assets, Precision Tax Relief (flat fee, Enrolled Agents) or Community Tax (lowest investigation fee) are the best value. For business tax problems (payroll, 941, trust fund recovery), Larson Tax Relief is the specialist. For complex cases with criminal exposure, Tax Court potential, or offshore issues, Fortress Tax Relief (attorney-only) is the fit. Our tax attorney vs CPA vs enrolled agent comparison walks through the specific problem shapes each professional handles best. Can I get my money back if a tax relief company doesn't help me? It depends on the firm's guarantee. Anthem Tax Services offers a 100% money-back guarantee on resolution work if they don't reduce your balance or restructure your payment plan (with specific exclusions). Optima Tax Relief has a 15-day money-back guarantee on the investigation phase only. Community Tax refunds the investigation fee if no resolution is possible. Precision Tax Relief has a satisfaction-focused approach but no explicit money-back guarantee posted. Larson and Fortress do not publish money-back guarantees. Read the specific guarantee terms carefully — many exclude bookkeeping, tax prep, and require a minimum deposit.                 }
              },
              {
                "@type": "Question",
                "name": "Are any tax relief companies backed by the IRS?",
                "acceptedAnswer": {
                  "@type": "Answer",
                  "text": "No. The IRS does not endorse, back, or accredit any private tax-relief company. Any firm that implies IRS endorsement is violating IRS Circular 230 and FTC advertising rules. The IRS's own consumer resources — irs.gov/payments — are free. Some firms use marketing language like 'Fresh Start Program' or 'IRS Approved' that trades on the IRS's actual Fresh Start Initiative (a real 2012 IRS policy change that expanded OIC criteria) but implies more IRS involvement than exists."
                }
              },
              {
                "@type": "Question",
                "name": "Which tax relief company should I use for my case?",
                "acceptedAnswer": {
                  "@type": "Answer",
                  "text": "Match the firm to your case shape. For balances under $50,000 with straightforward W-2 income, do it yourself through the IRS Online Payment Agreement — no firm needed. For Offer in Compromise cases with W-2 income and modest assets, Precision Tax Relief (flat fee, Enrolled Agents) or Community Tax (lowest investigation fee) are the best value. For business tax problems (payroll, 941, trust fund recovery), Larson Tax Relief is the specialist. For complex cases with criminal exposure, Tax Court potential, or offshore issues, Fortress Tax Relief (attorney-only) is the fit. Our tax attorney vs CPA vs enrolled agent comparison walks through the specific problem shapes each professional handles best."
                }
              },
              {
                "@type": "Question",
                "name": "Can I get my money back if a tax relief company doesn't help me?",
                "acceptedAnswer": {
                  "@type": "Answer",
                  "text": "It depends on the firm's guarantee. Anthem Tax Services offers a 100% money-back guarantee on resolution work if they don't reduce your balance or restructure your payment plan (with specific exclusions). Optima Tax Relief has a 15-day money-back guarantee on the investigation phase only. Community Tax refunds the investigation fee if no resolution is possible. Precision Tax Relief has a satisfaction-focused approach but no explicit money-back guarantee posted. Larson and Fortress do not publish money-back guarantees. Read the specific guarantee terms carefully — many exclude bookkeeping, tax prep, and require a minimum deposit."
                }

          if (!vid) { vid = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 12) + Date.now().toString(36); localStorage.setItem('_tu_vid', vid); }
        } catch (e) { /* storage blocked — send without an id */ }
        function send(event, root, trigger) {
          var body = JSON.stringify({ event: event, tool: root.getAttribute('data-tool'), path: location.pathname, vid: vid, trigger: trigger || '' });
          try {
            if (navigator.sendBeacon && navigator.sendBeacon(API, new Blob([body], { type: 'text/plain' }))) return;

        var viewed = false;
        function checkView() {
          if (viewed) return;
          var root = document.querySelector('[data-tool]');

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', checkView); else checkView();

          var el = e.target;
          if (!el || !el.closest) return;
          var root = el.closest('[data-tool]');
          if (!root) return;
          if (e.type === 'click' && !root.hasAttribute('data-tool-any-click') && !el.closest(CONTROLS)) return;
          used = true;
          if (!viewed) { viewed = true; send('view', root); }
          send('use', root, e.type);
        }
        ['click', 'input', 'change', 'submit'].forEach(function (t) {
          document.addEventListener(t, onEvent, true);
        });
      })();
    </script><link rel="icon" href="/favicon.ico" sizes="any"><link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="/favicon.svg"><link rel="icon" type="image/png" sizes="48x48" href="/favicon-48.png"><link rel="icon" type="image/png" sizes="192x192" href="/favicon-192.png"><link rel="apple-touch-icon" sizes="180x180" href="/apple-touch-icon.png"><meta name="generator" content="Astro v4.16.19"><title>Best Tax Relief Companies 2026: Verified Picks</title><meta name="description" content="The best tax relief companies of 2026, ranked by BBB record, staff credentials, pricing transparency, and FTC-flagged issues. They are usually not worth it for straightforward cases: streamlined online installment agreements (balance ≤ $50,000) cost $22 through the IRS Online Payment Agreement, and First Time Abate can be requested by phone. The FTC warns that most taxpayers don't qualify for the aggressive settlements these companies advertise, so run your specific numbers through our Offer in Compromise calculator before you hire anyone."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Is IRS tax relief legit?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"IRS tax relief itself is real — Offer in Compromise, installment agreements, Currently Not Collectible status, and penalty abatement are all statutory programs the IRS administers under the Internal Revenue Code. The FY2025 IRS Data Book shows 7,199 of 33,591 offers were accepted (21.4%). Tax-relief companies that help clients access these programs are legitimate businesses. What isn't legitimate: firms promising 'pennies on the dollar' before ever seeing your Form 433 financials, or firms that charge upfront fees before doing any work. The FTC's Tax Relief Companies alert covers the specific patterns to avoid."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"How much does a tax relief company cost?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Cost depends on the case type and the firm's pricing model. An Offer in Compromise typically runs $3,000–$7,500 (Enrolled Agents and CPAs at the lower end, tax attorneys at the higher end). A Partial Pay Installment Agreement runs $1,500–$3,500. A First Time Abate request runs $500–$1,500 (though for a First Time Abate, doing it yourself with a phone call to the IRS is usually the right call). Investigation phase fees (used by Community Tax and Optima) start around $295–$549 for individual cases. Flat-fee firms like Precision Tax quote after a free eligibility assessment."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Are any tax relief companies backed by the IRS?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"No. The IRS does not endorse, back, or accredit any private tax-relief company. Any firm that implies IRS endorsement is violating IRS Circular 230 and FTC advertising rules. The IRS's own consumer resources — irs.gov/payments — are free. Some firms use marketing language like 'Fresh Start Program' or 'IRS Approved' that trades on the IRS's actual Fresh Start Initiative (a real 2012 IRS policy change that expanded OIC criteria) but implies more IRS involvement than exists."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Which tax relief company should I use for my case?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Match the firm to your case shape. For balances under $50,000 with straightforward W-2 income, do it yourself through the IRS Online Payment Agreement — no firm needed. For Offer in Compromise cases with W-2 income and modest assets, Precision Tax Relief (flat fee, Enrolled Agents) or Community Tax (lowest investigation fee) are the best value. For business tax problems (payroll, 941, trust fund recovery), Larson Tax Relief is the specialist. For complex cases with criminal exposure, Tax Court potential, or offshore issues, Fortress Tax Relief (attorney-only) is the fit. Our tax attorney vs CPA vs enrolled agent comparison walks through the specific problem shapes each professional handles best."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Can I get my money back if a tax relief company doesn't help me?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"It depends on the firm's guarantee. Anthem Tax Services offers a 100% money-back guarantee on resolution work if they don't reduce your balance or restructure your payment plan (with specific exclusions). Optima Tax Relief has a 15-day money-back guarantee on the investigation phase only. Community Tax refunds the investigation fee if no resolution is possible. Precision Tax Relief has a satisfaction-focused approach but no explicit money-back guarantee posted. Larson and Fortress do not publish money-back guarantees. Read the specific guarantee terms carefully — many exclude bookkeeping, tax prep, and require a minimum deposit."}}]}</script><script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"Person","name":"Jonathan Velez","url":"https://www.themodernwallet.com/about/","jobTitle":"Personal Finance Writer & Editor","description":"Jonathan Velez writes and edits ModernWallet's personal-finance content, focused on loans, interest, and debt payoff.
